Ayat 31:29 and 31:33 then made the “later” horizon structurally active through appointed term and firm promise.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000019/B001","root_001273/B008","root_001696/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_21fbe00596e8e5cb9bf9","text":"2. **Present uprightness held by the later horizon.** **ق و م B008** supplies straightness and evenness; **ء خ ر B001** supplies what comes later; **ي ق ن B001** is settled knowledge with doubt removed. The emphatic repeated *hum* places certainty as the stable posture behind the two actions. I infer that a settled later horizon holds present conduct upright. This adds temporal mechanics: future certainty is functioning now as the support of regular practice.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000019/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2de79d57a8ca4bc7f987","text":"2. **The later end exerting force on present conduct.** Lexical evidence: `ء خ ر B001` is what comes later, `ي ق ن B001` is settled knowledge with doubt removed, and `ق و م B003` is resolve to undertake a matter. Structural cue: the doubled pronoun frame, `وَهُم ... هُمْ`, encloses certainty, while 31:9 supplies enduring residence and a true promise. Assigned role: certainty about the later end anchors the repeated present-tense practices. Abductive move (inferred): settled knowledge of an outcome supplies resolve before that outcome arrives. Reading change: belief in the afterlife is not a third item after ritual and giving; it is the temporal force that keeps both standing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3fcb9e3cc687f78e4aea","text":"- 31:8's gardens made the growth branch of `ز ك و` materially visible, and 31:9's enduring true promise made the afterlife clause function retrospectively as the time-horizon sustaining the two present practices.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000009/B002","root_000637/B001","root_000879/B003","root_001273/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_45d94f7243a39ff9be08","text":"1. **A maintained cycle of worship and social growth.** **ق و م B004** contributes care, guardianship, and preservation to *yuqīmūna*; **ص ل و B003** is prescribed worship; **ء ت ي B002** is granting; **ز ك و B001–B002** is growth and purity. The sequence maintenance → worship → transfer → growth makes the practices mutually sustaining. I infer that what is granted enables communal increase and rectification. The ayah reads as upkeep of a living devotional-social system, not two isolated observances.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000009/B002","root_000637/B001","root_000637/B002","root_000879/B003","root_001273/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4cdb92108be5cc52b8ca","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"31:4:2\",\"root_001273\",[\"B004\"]],[\"31:4:3\",\"root_000879\",[\"B003\"]],[\"31:4:4\",\"root_000009\",[\"B002\"]],[\"31:4:5\",\"root_000637\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Namazın sürdürülmesiyle zekâtın verilmesi, ibadeti, aktarımı, arınmayı ve toplumsal gelişmeyi birbirine bağlayan canlı bir bakım düzeni kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000009/B002","root_000637/B001","root_000879/B003","root_001273/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_500186aa0c2adfd214a7","text":"1. **A maintained cycle of worship, transfer, and growth.** Lexical evidence: `ق و م B004` is care, maintenance, and preservation; `ص ل و B003` is prescribed worship; `ء ت ي B002` is granting; and `ز ك و B001/B002` combines growth with purification. Structural cue: the two imperfect verbs are coordinated, and 31:5 names `ف ل ح`, whose `B003` branch is the tiller, before 31:8 introduces gardens. Assigned role: maintained prayer stabilizes the cycle, while transferred zakah circulates what can purify and grow. Abductive move (inferred): the agricultural echoes imply that regular maintenance and giving cultivate the social-moral field. Reading change: the paired practices read as a regenerative system rather than two independent checklist items.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000019/B001","root_001273/B003","root_001273/B008","root_001696/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b916c33787abc9338009","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"31:4:2\",\"root_001273\",[\"B003\",\"B008\"]],[\"31:4:7\",\"root_000019\",[\"B001\"]],[\"31:4:9\",\"root_001696\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ahirete ilişkin sarsılmaz bilgi, gelecekteki sonucu şimdiki davranışı ayakta tutan etkin bir dayanağa dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
